    function ChgTab(tab) {

		var contentColor = 'col3 px-sm-2 hp_' + tab + '_Content';

        divCard.className = 'card hp_' + tab + '_TopBorder';
        divCardHead.className = 'col3 p-1 header hp_' + tab + '_Header';

        divMBlnk.className   = (tab == 'mb') ? contentColor + ' hp_mb_Selected' : contentColor;
        divCElnk.className   = (tab == 'ce') ? contentColor + ' hp_ce_Selected' : contentColor;
        divMAGlnk.className  = (tab == 'mag') ? contentColor + ' hp_mag_Selected' : contentColor;
        divNEWSlnk.className = (tab == 'news') ? contentColor + ' hp_news_Selected' : contentColor;

        divMB.style.display  = (tab == 'mb')? 'block' : 'none';
        divCE.style.display  = (tab == 'ce')? 'block' : 'none';
        divMAG.style.display = (tab == 'mag')? 'block' : 'none';
        divNEW.style.display = (tab == 'news') ? 'block' : 'none';

        return false;
    }

</script>

<script>

    function saveNewsletter(memberID) {

        //dont have to validate if already logged in
        var loggedIn = (memberID != 0);
        var txtEmail = "";
        var pageValid = true;

        if (!loggedIn) {

            txtEmail = document.getElementById("ctl00_ctl00_NewsSignUp_txtEmailAddress").value;

            if (txtEmail == "") {
                spanErr.innerHTML = "Email Address Required";
                return false;
            }

            var regex = /([0-9a-zA-Z]([-.\w]*[0-9a-zA-Z])*@(([0-9a-zA-Z])+([-\w]*[0-9a-zA-Z])*\.)+[a-zA-Z]{2,9})$/;
            pageValid = regex.test(txtEmail)

            if (!pageValid) {
                spanErr.innerHTML = "Invalid Email Address";
                return false;
            }
        }

        var email = "";

        if (loggedIn)
            email = document.getElementById("ctl00_ctl00_NewsSignUp_lbEmail").innerHTML;
        else
            email = txtEmail;

        var xml = "<newsletters>";
        xml = xml + "<news id='" + 2 + "' />";
        xml = xml + "</newsletters>";

        //save newsletter subscription
        FMWS.WebService.NewsletterSignup(email, xml, memberID);
        document.getElementById("ctl00_ctl00_NewsSignUp_lbMessage").innerHTML = 'You have been successully subscribed.<br />Thank you for your support!';
        document.getElementById("ctl00_ctl00_NewsSignUp_pNewsletter").style.display = 'none';

    }

</script>
